davidbmar.comSearchProjectsDailyClusters

Daily

A headline digest of what changed across the portfolio, by day.

Deep dives

In-depth applied tutorials, grouped by theme.

  • Weather Integration and Global PreferencesYou can now persist user preferences like 'home_city' across sessions and safely fetch weather data. The flow engine handles missing state by prompting the user before calling external APIs.
  • Grounding Integrity GateYou now enforce deterministic flow grounding via the Grounding Integrity Gate and route calls by number-specific context. You validate flows across L1-L4 levels using the centralized JsonMaintenanceRequestStore.
  • Audio-gated flows and flow picker improvementsYou unblock text-mode flows by simulating audio completion for read-only steps and hiding invalid config partials from the picker.
  • Alibaba DashScope/Qwen IntegrationYou swap the default utterance generator from Anthropic Haiku to Alibaba Qwen using a lightweight HTTP client. This cuts costs and dependencies while preserving Anthropic as a fallback option.
  • Flow EvaluationYou can now automate agent flow grading using a persona-driven driver and an LLM judge. This setup replaces manual checks with consistent, scored reports for success and quality.

Build a Deterministic Voice AI Testing Harness with Synthetic Callers

This update introduces a generic, event-driven synthetic caller harness that drives voice flows to completion, enabling deterministic regression testing and coverage of edge cases like confirmation gates. It also implements a two-layer nightly audio review cycle and fixes critical VAD and state-management bugs to improve live call reliability.

github-portfolio-search 2riff 40
▸ how to apply & commit details

How you can apply this

  • Implement an event-driven synthetic caller (voice_case.py) that drives any voice flow to a terminal state, allowing you to automate end-to-end testing of complex conversational paths without manual intervention.
  • Adopt a 'shift-left' linter strategy by flagging logical errors like 'announce-booked-without-booking' in CI, preventing invalid states from reaching production and reducing debugging time.
  • Wire a two-layer nightly audio review cycle using remote Whisper APIs with local fallbacks to automatically populate a quality database, creating a measurable feedback loop for voice model performance.
  • Fix VAD tuning and state transitions to handle short inputs like 'yes' without excessive pauses, ensuring natural conversation flow and preventing stalls during confirmation gates.

Builds LLM Policy Engine with Per-State Routing and Certification Matrix

Implemented a robust LLM policy framework featuring per-state routing, a 'judge-the-judge' audit system, and automated certification matrices. Enhanced voice interaction quality through DashScope streaming integration and refined slot-handling logic for natural conversational flows.

github-portfolio-search 6riff 33
▸ how to apply & commit details

How you can apply this

  • Implement per-state LLM routing using a flow_llm_map to dynamically assign models based on conversation context, enabling cost and performance optimization.
  • Adopt a 'judge-the-judge' spot-audit mechanism where a powerful model reviews a subset of outputs from cheaper models to ensure quality without full overhead.
  • Automate model certification by building a matrix builder that tests specific states against defined personas, shifting quality assurance left in the development cycle.
  • Reduce conversational friction by passing slot labels to read-back guards and refining reprompt phrasing to prevent leaking internal slot names to users.

Build a Crossed-Panel LLM Eval Matrix with Automated Quality Gates

Implemented a comprehensive evaluation framework featuring a 3-agent by 3-judge crossed panel, automated CI quality gates, and a SQLite-backed performance model. The system integrates computed operational metrics with judged aspects like humanness to detect regressions and backend stalls.

github-portfolio-search 2riff 55
▸ how to apply & commit details

How you can apply this

  • Implement a crossed-panel evaluation design (multiple agents × multiple judges) to decompose variance and isolate judge noise from model performance.
  • Use automated pre-push hooks and CI gates to block commits that cause regression in specific computed aspects like latency or completion rates.
  • Store evaluation scores in a structured SQLite database tracking changes over time, enabling trend analysis and rescore capabilities without re-running full suites.
  • Apply concreteness gates to scheduling flows by parsing natural language time expressions and blocking bookings on vague inputs before state transitions.

2026-05-28451 commits

Demo-Critical Agent Ops for Trouble-Ticket Hardening

The day centered on coordinating a demo-critical RIFF trouble-ticket path through agent-router pings, utilization snapshots, escalation relays, dashboard evidence, and Phase 2 standby tracking. RIFF added live-mic support, property-ticket routing and gate fixes, phone guards, red tests, generated trouble-ticket corpora, and run-by-run release evidence.

agent-router 406riff 45
▸ how to apply & commit details

How you can apply this

  • Use durable ack, standup, and ops/acks files as reusable cross-host relays when agent channels or review handoffs are unreliable.
  • Track multi-agent utilization with recurring snapshots, strict gates, and adoption-discount filters so standby noise does not look like real work.
  • Harden demo paths by pairing fixes with red E2E captures, generated scenario corpora, and release-criteria docs for each verification run.
  • Build operator dashboards from structured cards and Playwright health loops, then keep link hygiene and private-repo leakage checks in the loop.

▸ how to apply & commit details

How you can apply this

  • Roll out graph features in layers: typed store and tests first, then dedup, ingestion, REST endpoints, MCP tools, and CLI commands on top.

  • For multi-project apps, centralize project config and wire it into stores, header routing, dynamic tabs, selectors, build variables, and deploy scripts.

Scheduling Agents Hardened From OAuth to Audio

▸ how to apply & commit details

How you can apply this

  • Use two-phase booking with persisted pending records, proposal-time overlap checks, confirm-time double-booking prevention, and alternative slots.

2026-03-27462 commits

Workspaces, Voice Calls, and RAG Workflows Hardened

The day centered on making everyone-ai workspace portals more reliable, with idle/reconnect UX, ECR credential refresh, fleet config cleanup, Playwright smoke/E2E coverage, and all 4 portals redeployed with 4/4 E2E passing. It also shipped a Telnyx/Kokoro phone agent with caller ID and lead capture, plus RAG tool v2.0 work spanning auth, performance, an AI domain wizard, docs, and verification tests.

everyone-ai 358phone-agent-keiko-tv 36tool-RAG-for-split-personalities 67traceable-searchable-adr-memory-index 1
▸ how to apply & commit details

How you can apply this

  • Reuse the workspace reliability pattern: expose honest idle/reconnect states, fetch live workspace status before promising preservation, and back it with Playwright launch/cleanup tests.
  • Apply fleet deployment hygiene: keep portal config explicit, remove stale services, refresh ECR credentials via CronJob/deploy scripts, and publish fleet status artifacts from test teardown.
  • Build phone-call UX around real-time identity: combine SQLite contacts, caller ID lookup, unknown-caller lead capture, pre-synthesized greetings, and 8kHz Telnyx/Kokoro audio streaming.
  • For RAG apps, pair major UX features with guardrails: JWT/user-scoped auth, caching/indexes/perf endpoints, AI persona wizard dry-runs, and release docs with verification tests.

2026-03-26243 commits

Terminal Ops, Log UX, and RAG Memory Land

everyone-ai shipped a deep operations polish pass: resilient web terminals, richer log streaming/search/download/copy flows, workspace/app filtering, stability indicators, and launch-state fixes. tool-RAG-for-split-personalities added complete RAG answer coverage, domain templates, conversation export/share APIs, server-side persistence, persona memory, resume UI, mobile polish, and large Playwright regression suites.

everyone-ai 162tool-RAG-for-split-personalities 81
▸ how to apply & commit details

How you can apply this

  • Reuse xterm SearchAddon, bracketed paste, copy-on-select, reconnect buttons, and single-registered listeners to make browser terminals feel durable.
  • Expose logs as SSE streams, then layer modal search, ANSI rendering/stripping, raw-text downloads, line counts, hover previews, and click-to-copy lines on top.
  • Make ops dashboards more reusable with pinned workspaces, status filters, degraded/restart indicators, DOM caching, reduced-motion media queries, and explicit launch error states.
  • For RAG apps, pair template galleries and export/share/list/delete endpoints with server-side conversations, persona memory APIs, resume UI, and Playwright suites covering core flows.
